// The registered values the scorer judges against; each one's basis is in the registration.
const (
	// DetectBoundSeconds is P1, an idealised bound: one 1.0 s exporter collection, one 1.0 s scrape period and
	// one 0.8 s scrape timeout, with collection, publication and scheduling taking no time and no scrape failing.
	DetectBoundSeconds = 1.0 + 1.0 + 0.8
	// WindowSeconds is W, the observation window from the end of the injection call.
	WindowSeconds = 120.0
	// MinValidReps is the least number of valid repetitions an arm is judged on.
	MinValidReps = 3
	// truncationSlack is how far below zero a hop may read before the single clock is contradicted: whole-second
	// Kubernetes fields are truncated, so a true order can read up to one second backwards.
	truncationSlack = 1.0
)

// The annotation and taint names the platform writes, as the controller defines them.
// They are repeated here, not imported, because the scorer reads evidence and must not depend on the code it judges.
const (
	annFingerprint   = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/fingerprint"
	annObservedAt    = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/observed-at"
	annTaintedAt     = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/tainted-at"
	annReading       = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/reading"
	annReadingAt     = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/reading-at"
	annDeactivatedAt = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/deactivated-at"
	annWLFingerprint = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/deactivated-fingerprint"
	annReactivatedAt = "gpufault.platform.lkhun9311.github.io/reactivated-at"
	taintKey         = "platform.lkhun9311.github.io/gpu-fault"
	condGPUHealthy   = "GPUHealthy"
)

// Arms, and the field and value each injects.
var armInjection = map[string]struct {
	field string
	value string
	fault bool
}{
	"xid":    {FieldXid, "79", true},
	"ecc":    {FieldECCDoubleBit, "1", true},
	"benign": {FieldECCSingleBit, "1", false},
	armNone:  {FieldXid, "", false},
}

// armNone is the arm that injects nothing, and so is read differently at every step.
const armNone = "none"

// Arms lists the arms in the registered order.
var Arms = []string{"xid", "ecc", "benign", armNone}

// streams are the watch files every repetition records, by the resource each watches.
var streams = []string{"node", "nodehealth", "workload", "job", "pods"}

// Interval is a duration in seconds, [Lo, Hi]: Lo == Hi only when both ends are nanosecond stamps.
type Interval struct {
	Lo float64 `json:"lo"`
	Hi float64 `json:"hi"`
}

// CellResult is one repetition's reading.
type CellResult struct {
	Dir     string               `json:"dir"`
	Arm     string               `json:"arm"`
	Rep     int                  `json:"rep"`
	Block   int                  `json:"block"`
	Mode    string               `json:"mode"`
	Invalid []string             `json:"invalid,omitempty"`
	Hops    map[string]*Interval `json:"hops,omitempty"`
	// Failed names each registered predicate a fault repetition broke, with what the evidence showed.
	Failed []string `json:"failed,omitempty"`
	// Actions lists the platform's actions in a control repetition.
	Actions []string `json:"actions,omitempty"`
	// AllocatableChanged is P5's reading: whether the target's allocatable GPUs moved across the injection.
	AllocatableChanged bool `json:"allocatableChanged"`
	// Cut is true when the session ran out of time inside this repetition.
	Cut bool `json:"cut,omitempty"`
}

// Valid reports whether the repetition is scored at all.
func (c CellResult) Valid() bool { return len(c.Invalid) == 0 && !c.Cut }

// readEnv reads a key=value file; a missing file is an error, a malformed line is an error.
func readEnv(path string) (map[string]string, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := map[string]string{}
	for i, line := range strings.Split(string(b), "\n") {
		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
		if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
			continue
		}
		k, v, ok := strings.Cut(line, "=")
		if !ok {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s:%d: no '=' in %q", path, i+1, line)
		}
		out[k] = v
	}
	return out, nil
}

// epoch parses the runner's `date +%s.%N` stamps.
func epoch(s string) (time.Time, error) {
	sec, frac, _ := strings.Cut(strings.TrimSpace(s), ".")
	si, err := strconv.ParseInt(sec, 10, 64)
	if err != nil || s == "" {
		return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("not an epoch stamp: %q", s)
	}
	var ns int64
	if frac != "" {
		frac = (frac + "000000000")[:9]
		if ns, err = strconv.ParseInt(frac, 10, 64); err != nil {
			return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("not an epoch stamp: %q", s)
		}
	}
	return time.Unix(si, ns).UTC(), nil
}

// decodeStream reads a `kubectl get -w -o json` file: concatenated objects, a List expanded into its items.
// An empty or missing file is an error, because a watch that recorded nothing is not a watch that saw nothing.
func decodeStream[T any](path string) ([]T, error) {
	b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(bytes.TrimSpace(b)) == 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s is empty", filepath.Base(path))
	}
	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(b))
	var out []T
	for {
		var raw json.RawMessage
		if err := dec.Decode(&raw); errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
			break
		} else if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", filepath.Base(path), err)
		}
		var head struct {
			Kind  string            `json:"kind"`
			Items []json.RawMessage `json:"items"`
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &head); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", filepath.Base(path), err)
		}
		docs := []json.RawMessage{raw}
		if strings.HasSuffix(head.Kind, "List") {
			docs = head.Items
		}
		for _, d := range docs {
			var v T
			if err := json.Unmarshal(d, &v); err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", filepath.Base(path), err)
			}
			out = append(out, v)
		}
	}
	return out, nil
}

// watchCoverage checks a watch's start and end records: one start before from, and no end before to.
//
// A watch that disconnected inside the window leaves a file that parses and is not empty, and whatever happened
// in the gap is unknown, not absent; so a gap makes the repetition invalid rather than quiet.
func watchCoverage(path string, from, to time.Time) error {
	f, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
	starts, firstStart := 0, time.Time{}
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(f)
	for sc.Scan() {
		fields := strings.Fields(sc.Text())
		if len(fields) < 2 {
			continue
		}
		t, err := epoch(fields[1])
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", filepath.Base(path), err)
		}
		switch fields[0] {
		case "start":
			starts++
			if firstStart.IsZero() {
				firstStart = t
			}
		case "end":
			if t.Before(to) {
				return fmt.Errorf("%s: the watch ended at %s, inside the window", filepath.Base(path), t.Format(time.RFC3339Nano))
			}
		}
	}
	switch {
	case starts == 0:
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: no watch start recorded", filepath.Base(path))
	case starts > 1:
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: the watch restarted %d times", filepath.Base(path), starts-1)
	case firstStart.After(from):
		return fmt.Errorf("%s: the watch started at %s, after the injection began", filepath.Base(path), firstStart.Format(time.RFC3339Nano))
	}
	return sc.Err()
}

func ann(o metav1.Object, key string) (time.Time, bool) {
	s := o.GetAnnotations()[key]
	if s == "" {
		return time.Time{}, false
	}
	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, s)
	return t, err == nil
}

func secs(d time.Duration) float64 { return d.Seconds() }

// span is end − start when end is a nanosecond stamp and start is the injection bracket [s0, s1].
func span(end, s0, s1 time.Time) *Interval {
	return &Interval{secs(end.Sub(s1)), secs(end.Sub(s0))}
}

// spanS is end − start when end is a whole-second Kubernetes time, truncated, and start is a bracket.
func spanS(end, s0, s1 time.Time) *Interval {
	return &Interval{secs(end.Sub(s1)), secs(end.Add(time.Second).Sub(s0))}
}

// wholeSecond is a nanosecond start to a truncated end: [end − start, end + 1 − start].
func wholeSecond(end, start time.Time) *Interval {
	d := secs(end.Sub(start))
	return &Interval{d, d + 1}
}

// bothWhole is the difference of two truncated times, each up to a second late.
func bothWhole(end, start time.Time) *Interval {
	d := secs(end.Sub(start))
	return &Interval{d - 1, d + 1}
}

// cellEvidence is a repetition's decoded streams.
type cellEvidence struct {
	nhs   []platformv1.NodeHealth
	nodes []corev1.Node
	wls   []kueuev1beta1.Workload
	jobs  []batchv1.Job
	pods  []corev1.Pod
}

func loadEvidence(dir string) (cellEvidence, error) {
	var e cellEvidence
	var errs [5]error
	e.nhs, errs[0] = decodeStream[platformv1.NodeHealth](filepath.Join(dir, "watch-nodehealth.json"))
	e.nodes, errs[1] = decodeStream[corev1.Node](filepath.Join(dir, "watch-node.json"))
	e.wls, errs[2] = decodeStream[kueuev1beta1.Workload](filepath.Join(dir, "watch-workload.json"))
	e.jobs, errs[3] = decodeStream[batchv1.Job](filepath.Join(dir, "watch-job.json"))
	e.pods, errs[4] = decodeStream[corev1.Pod](filepath.Join(dir, "watch-pods.json"))
	return e, errors.Join(errs[:]...)
}

// ScoreCell reads one repetition's directory.
func ScoreCell(dir string) (CellResult, error) {
	env, err := readEnv(filepath.Join(dir, "cell.env"))

exec
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '300,900p' internal/gpufault/score.go; cat internal/controller/nodehealth_gpufault_test.go" in /home/lkhun9311/workspace/gpu-platform-control-plane/.claude/worktrees/agent-a5b6768413de6a7da
 succeeded in 0ms:
	env, err := readEnv(filepath.Join(dir, "cell.env"))
	if err != nil {
		return CellResult{}, err
	}
	res := CellResult{Dir: dir, Arm: env["arm"], Mode: env["mode"], Hops: map[string]*Interval{}}
	res.Rep, _ = strconv.Atoi(env["rep"])
	res.Block, _ = strconv.Atoi(env["block"])
	inj, ok := armInjection[res.Arm]
	if !ok {
		return CellResult{}, fmt.Errorf("%s: arm %q is not registered", dir, res.Arm)
	}
	invalid := func(f string, a ...any) { res.Invalid = append(res.Invalid, fmt.Sprintf(f, a...)) }
	res.Cut = env["cut"] == "true"

	// V6, the part a cell can see: the run must say which exporter it read.
	if res.Mode != "fake" && res.Mode != "dcgm" {
		invalid("V6: mode %q is neither fake nor dcgm", res.Mode)
	}
	// V2.
	if env["pre_state"] != "ok" {
		invalid("V2: pre-state %q", env["pre_state"])
	}
	tInj0, e1 := epoch(env["t_inject_start"])
	tInj1, e2 := epoch(env["t_inject_end"])
	tEnd, e3 := epoch(env["t_window_end"])
	if err := errors.Join(e1, e2, e3); err != nil || tInj1.Before(tInj0) {
		invalid("V3: injection or window stamps unreadable or out of order (%v)", err)
		return res, nil
	}
	// V1: the value read back is the value written, and the field is the arm's.
	wantRB := inj.value
	if res.Arm == armNone {
		wantRB = env["baseline"]
	}
	if env["readback"] != wantRB || wantRB == "" {
		invalid("V1: readback %q, want %q", env["readback"], wantRB)
	}
	if env["field"] != fieldID(inj.field) {
		invalid("V1: field %q, want %s", env["field"], fieldID(inj.field))
	}
	res.AllocatableChanged = env["alloc_before"] != env["alloc_after"]

	// V5: the exporter served the injected value, for every arm that injected.
	if res.Arm != armNone {
		first, perr := firstProbe(filepath.Join(dir, "probe.tsv"), inj.value, tInj0, tEnd)
		switch {
		case perr != nil:
			invalid("V3: probe: %v", perr)
		case first.IsZero():
			invalid("V5: the exporter never served %s=%s inside the window", inj.field, inj.value)
		default:
			res.Hops["exporter_lag"] = span(first, tInj0, tInj1)
		}
	}

	// V3: every stream present, parseable, and watching from before the injection to the window's end.
	for _, s := range streams {
		if err := watchCoverage(filepath.Join(dir, "watch-"+s+".log"), tInj0, tEnd); err != nil {
			invalid("V3: %v", err)
		}
	}
	ev, err := loadEvidence(dir)
	if err != nil {
		invalid("V3: %v", err)
		return res, nil
	}
	job, target, healthy := env["job"], env["target"], env["healthy"]
	ev.nhs = slices.DeleteFunc(ev.nhs, func(n platformv1.NodeHealth) bool { return n.Spec.NodeName != target })
	ev.nodes = slices.DeleteFunc(ev.nodes, func(n corev1.Node) bool { return n.Name != target })
	ev.wls = slices.DeleteFunc(ev.wls, func(w kueuev1beta1.Workload) bool { return !ownedByJob(&w, job) })
	ev.jobs = slices.DeleteFunc(ev.jobs, func(j batchv1.Job) bool { return j.Name != job })
	if len(ev.nhs) == 0 || len(ev.nodes) == 0 {
		invalid("V3: the streams carry no NodeHealth or Node for target %q", target)
		return res, nil
	}

	if !inj.fault {
		scoreControl(&res, env, ev, inj.field, tInj0, tEnd, invalid)
		return res, nil
	}
	scoreFault(&res, env, ev, inj.field, inj.value, tInj0, tInj1, target, healthy)
	return res, nil
}

// scoreControl counts the platform's actions in a control window, and demands evidence the observer was reading.
//
// A quiet control is evidence only if the operator was reading the card: for the benign arm, the reading it
// recorded must carry the injected value; for no injection, it must have recorded the sentinel the runner
// injects after the window. Without that, the repetition says nothing and is invalid.
func scoreControl(res *CellResult, env map[string]string, ev cellEvidence, field string, tInj0, tEnd time.Time,
	invalid func(string, ...any)) {
	res.Actions = controlActions(ev)
	want, from := env["uuid"]+"/"+field+"=1", tInj0
	if res.Arm == armNone {
		t, err := epoch(env["t_sentinel_start"])
		if err != nil || env["sentinel_value"] == "" {
			invalid("V7: no sentinel recorded, so nothing shows the observer was reading (%v)", err)
			return
		}
		want, from = env["uuid"]+"/"+FieldECCSingleBit+"="+env["sentinel_value"], t
		tEnd = t.Add(SentinelSeconds * time.Second)
	}
	for i := range ev.nhs {
		at, ok := ann(&ev.nhs[i], annReadingAt)
		if ok && !at.Before(from) && !at.After(tEnd) && slices.Contains(strings.Split(ev.nhs[i].Annotations[annReading], ","), want) {
			return
		}
	}
	invalid("V7: the operator never recorded reading %s between %s and %s", want, from.Format(time.RFC3339Nano), tEnd.Format(time.RFC3339Nano))
}

// SentinelSeconds is how long after the no-injection window the runner waits for the operator to read its
// sentinel: ten scrape periods, a convention.
const SentinelSeconds = 10

// faultTimes are a fault repetition's hop times, each zero until its evidence is found.
type faultTimes struct {
	obs, taint, deact, evict, release, react, readmit, suspend, stop, start0, placed, run time.Time
	wrongNode                                                                             string
}

// scoreFault reads a fault repetition's hops, in the order each stream recorded them.
func scoreFault(res *CellResult, env map[string]string, ev cellEvidence, field, value string, tInj0, tInj1 time.Time,
	target, healthy string) {
	fail := func(f string, a ...any) { res.Failed = append(res.Failed, fmt.Sprintf(f, a...)) }
	want := env["uuid"] + "/" + field + "=" + value
	var ft faultTimes
	if latched := ft.readNodeHealth(ev.nhs, want); latched != "" {
		// The stimulus is established independently, by readback and the probe; a different fingerprint is the
		// platform's output, and a wrong output is a result.
		fail("attribution: the platform latched %q, the injection was %q", latched, want)
		return
	}
	nodeTainted := slices.ContainsFunc(ev.nodes, func(n corev1.Node) bool {
		return slices.ContainsFunc(n.Spec.Taints, func(t corev1.Taint) bool { return t.Key == taintKey })
	})
	if !ft.taint.IsZero() && !nodeTainted {
		fail("P2-order: tainted-at is recorded and no Node object in the stream carries the taint")
	}
	ft.readWorkload(ev.wls, want)
	ft.readJob(ev.jobs)
	ft.readPods(ev.pods, target, healthy)
	if ft.wrongNode != "" {
		fail("P3-wrong-node: %s was placed after the taint, not on the healthy node", ft.wrongNode)
	}
	if missing := ft.firstMissing(); missing != "" {
		fail("P2-missing: no %s", missing)
	}
	ft.hops(res, tInj0, tInj1)
	if !ft.obs.IsZero() && ft.obs.Before(tInj0) {
		fail("P2-order: observed-at precedes the injection's start")
	}
	if e := res.Hops["end_to_end"]; e != nil && e.Hi > WindowSeconds {
		fail("P3-late: placed at [%.3f, %.3f] s, not wholly inside W", e.Lo, e.Hi)
	}
	for _, f := range ft.orderFailures() {
		fail("%s", f)
	}
	for name, h := range res.Hops {
		if h.Hi < -truncationSlack {
			fail("P2-order: hop %s reads %.3f s", name, h.Hi)
		}
	}
}

// readNodeHealth finds the platform's own stamps; it returns a latched fingerprint that is not want, if any.
func (ft *faultTimes) readNodeHealth(nhs []platformv1.NodeHealth, want string) string {
	for i := range nhs {
		fp := nhs[i].Annotations[annFingerprint]
		switch {
		case fp == "":
			continue
		case fp != want:
			return fp
		}
		if ft.obs.IsZero() {
			ft.obs, _ = ann(&nhs[i], annObservedAt)
		}
		if t, ok := ann(&nhs[i], annTaintedAt); ok && ft.taint.IsZero() {
			ft.taint = t
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// readWorkload reads the Workload's transitions in stream order: deactivated, evicted, quota released,
// reactivated, readmitted. Stream order, not timestamps, because whole-second stamps can reorder within a second.
func (ft *faultTimes) readWorkload(wls []kueuev1beta1.Workload, want string) {
	released := false
	for i := range wls {
		w := &wls[i]
		if ft.deact.IsZero() {
			t, ok := ann(w, annDeactivatedAt)
			if !ok || w.Annotations[annWLFingerprint] != want {
				continue
			}
			ft.deact = t
		}
		if c := meta.FindStatusCondition(w.Status.Conditions, kueuev1beta1.WorkloadEvicted); ft.evict.IsZero() &&
			c != nil && c.Status == metav1.ConditionTrue && strings.HasPrefix(c.Reason, kueuev1beta1.WorkloadDeactivated) {
			ft.evict = c.LastTransitionTime.Time
		}
		qr := meta.FindStatusCondition(w.Status.Conditions, kueuev1beta1.WorkloadQuotaReserved)
		reserved := qr != nil && qr.Status == metav1.ConditionTrue
		switch {
		case !released && !reserved:
			released = true
			if qr != nil {
				ft.release = qr.LastTransitionTime.Time
			}
		case released && reserved && ft.readmit.IsZero():
			ft.readmit = qr.LastTransitionTime.Time
		}
		if t, ok := ann(w, annReactivatedAt); ok && ft.react.IsZero() {
			ft.react = t
		}
	}
}

// readJob finds the Job's suspension after the deactivation.
func (ft *faultTimes) readJob(jobs []batchv1.Job) {
	if ft.deact.IsZero() {
		return
	}
	for i := range jobs {
		for _, c := range jobs[i].Status.Conditions {
			if c.Type == batchv1.JobSuspended && c.Status == corev1.ConditionTrue && ft.suspend.IsZero() &&
				!c.LastTransitionTime.Time.Before(ft.deact.Truncate(time.Second)) {
				ft.suspend = c.LastTransitionTime.Time
			}
		}
	}
}

// readPods finds the displaced container's stop, the new Pod's placement, and any Pod placed on the wrong node.
func (ft *faultTimes) readPods(pods []corev1.Pod, target, healthy string) {
	for i := range pods {
		p := &pods[i]
		for _, cs := range p.Status.ContainerStatuses {
			if p.Spec.NodeName == target && cs.State.Terminated != nil && ft.stop.IsZero() {
				ft.stop, ft.start0 = cs.State.Terminated.FinishedAt.Time, cs.State.Terminated.StartedAt.Time
			}
			if p.Spec.NodeName == healthy && cs.State.Running != nil && ft.run.IsZero() {
				ft.run = cs.State.Running.StartedAt.Time
			}
		}
		c := podCondition(p, corev1.PodScheduled)
		if c == nil || c.Status != corev1.ConditionTrue {
			continue
		}
		afterTaint := !ft.taint.IsZero() && p.CreationTimestamp.After(ft.taint.Truncate(time.Second))
		switch {
		case p.Spec.NodeName == healthy && ft.placed.IsZero():
			ft.placed = c.LastTransitionTime.Time
		case p.Spec.NodeName != healthy && afterTaint:
			ft.wrongNode = p.Name + " on " + p.Spec.NodeName
		}
	}
}

// firstMissing names the first hop, in the path's order, with no evidence.
func (ft *faultTimes) firstMissing() string {
	for _, h := range []struct {
		name string
		at   time.Time
	}{{"observed", ft.obs}, {"tainted", ft.taint}, {"deactivated", ft.deact}, {"evicted", ft.evict}, {"suspended", ft.suspend},
		{"released", ft.release}, {"stopped", ft.stop}, {"reactivated", ft.react}, {"readmitted", ft.readmit}, {"placed", ft.placed}} {
		if h.at.IsZero() {
			return h.name
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// hops turns the times into the registered intervals; a hop whose ends are not both known is left out.
func (ft *faultTimes) hops(res *CellResult, tInj0, tInj1 time.Time) {
	exact := func(a, b time.Time) *Interval { d := secs(b.Sub(a)); return &Interval{d, d} }
	set := func(name string, ok bool, iv func() *Interval) {
		if ok {
			res.Hops[name] = iv()
		}
	}
	set("detect", !ft.obs.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return span(ft.obs, tInj0, tInj1) })
	set("taint", !ft.obs.IsZero() && !ft.taint.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return exact(ft.obs, ft.taint) })
	set("evict_request", !ft.taint.IsZero() && !ft.deact.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return exact(ft.taint, ft.deact) })
	for name, t := range map[string]time.Time{"kueue_evict": ft.evict, "job_suspend": ft.suspend, "quota_release": ft.release, "stop": ft.stop} {
		set(name, !ft.deact.IsZero() && !t.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return wholeSecond(t, ft.deact) })
	}
	set("readmit", !ft.release.IsZero() && !ft.readmit.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return bothWhole(ft.readmit, ft.release) })
	set("end_to_end", !ft.placed.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return spanS(ft.placed, tInj0, tInj1) })
	set("running", !ft.run.IsZero(), func() *Interval { return spanS(ft.run, tInj0, tInj1) })
	set("lost_work", !ft.stop.IsZero() && !ft.start0.IsZero(), func() *Interval {
		lw := bothWhole(ft.stop, ft.start0)
		lw.Lo = math.Max(0, lw.Lo)
		return lw
	})
}

// orderFailures checks P2's order among the nanosecond stamps, and against the truncated ones within truncation.
func (ft *faultTimes) orderFailures() []string {
	var out []string
	for _, o := range []struct {
		a, b   time.Time
		an, bn string
		slack  time.Duration
	}{
		{ft.obs, ft.taint, "observed", "tainted", 0}, {ft.taint, ft.deact, "tainted", "deactivated", 0},
		{ft.deact, ft.evict, "deactivated", "evicted", time.Second}, {ft.evict, ft.readmit, "evicted", "readmitted", time.Second},
		{ft.readmit, ft.placed, "readmitted", "placed", time.Second},
	} {
		if !o.a.IsZero() && !o.b.IsZero() && o.b.Add(o.slack).Before(o.a) {
			out = append(out, fmt.Sprintf("P2-order: %s at %s precedes %s at %s", o.bn, o.b.Format(time.RFC3339Nano), o.an, o.a.Format(time.RFC3339Nano)))
		}
	}
	return out
}

func fieldID(name string) string {
	switch name {
	case FieldXid:
		return "230"
	case FieldECCDoubleBit:
		return "311"
	case FieldECCSingleBit:
		return "310"
	}
	return ""
}

func ownedByJob(w *kueuev1beta1.Workload, job string) bool {
	for _, o := range w.OwnerReferences {
		if o.Kind == "Job" && o.Name == job {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func podCondition(p *corev1.Pod, t corev1.PodConditionType) *corev1.PodCondition {
	for i := range p.Status.Conditions {
		if p.Status.Conditions[i].Type == t {
			return &p.Status.Conditions[i]
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// controlActions lists every platform action a control repetition's streams carry.
func controlActions(ev cellEvidence) []string {
	var out []string
	for i := range ev.nhs {
		if c := meta.FindStatusCondition(ev.nhs[i].Status.Conditions, condGPUHealthy); c != nil && c.Status == metav1.ConditionFalse {
			out = append(out, "GPUHealthy=False ("+c.Reason+")")
			break
		}
	}
	for i := range ev.nhs {
		if fp := ev.nhs[i].Annotations[annFingerprint]; fp != "" {
			out = append(out, "latched "+fp)
			break
		}
	}
	for i := range ev.nodes {
		if slices.ContainsFunc(ev.nodes[i].Spec.Taints, func(t corev1.Taint) bool { return t.Key == taintKey }) {
			out = append(out, "gpu-fault taint")
			break
		}
	}
	for i := range ev.wls {
		if ev.wls[i].Annotations[annDeactivatedAt] != "" {
			out = append(out, "deactivated "+ev.wls[i].Name)
			break
		}
	}
	return out
}

// firstProbe returns the first probe sample inside [after, until] that carries the value.
// Each line is "<epoch>\t<value>"; a value of ERR is a probe that failed, and is skipped.
func firstProbe(path, value string, after, until time.Time) (time.Time, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
	want, err := strconv.ParseFloat(value, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("injected value %q is not a number", value)
	}
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(f)
	n := 0
	for sc.Scan() {
		ts, v, ok := strings.Cut(sc.Text(), "\t")
		if !ok {
			return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("malformed probe line %q", sc.Text())
		}
		n++
		t, err := epoch(ts)
		if err != nil {
			return time.Time{}, err
		}
		if t.Before(after) || t.After(until) || v == "ERR" {
			continue
		}
		if fv, err := strconv.ParseFloat(v, 64); err == nil && fv == want {
			return t, nil
		}
	}
	if err := sc.Err(); err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, err
	}
	if n == 0 {
		return time.Time{}, fmt.Errorf("the probe recorded no samples")
	}
	return time.Time{}, nil
}

// ArmVerdict is one arm's registered reading.
type ArmVerdict struct {
	Arm     string                `json:"arm"`
	Verdict string                `json:"verdict"`
	Valid   int                   `json:"valid"`
	Total   int                   `json:"total"`
	Reasons []string              `json:"reasons,omitempty"`
	Summary map[string][3]float64 `json:"summary,omitempty"`
}

// CheckResult is one registered check outside the arms: the stale-signal cells.
type CheckResult struct {
	Name    string   `json:"name"`
	Verdict string   `json:"verdict"`
	Reasons []string `json:"reasons,omitempty"`
}

// Report is the session's reading.
type Report struct {
	Label  string        `json:"label"`
	Arms   []ArmVerdict  `json:"arms"`
	Checks []CheckResult `json:"checks"`
	P5     string        `json:"p5"`
	Cells  []CellResult  `json:"cells"`
	Refuse []string      `json:"refuse,omitempty"`
}

// Judge applies the verdict rules to every repetition of a session; notRun names arms the smoke cell stopped.
func Judge(cells []CellResult, notRun map[string]bool) Report {
	rep := Report{Label: "RESULT", Cells: cells}
	modes := map[string]bool{}
	for _, c := range cells {
		modes[c.Mode] = true
	}
	switch {
	case len(cells) == 0:
		rep.Label = "NO-EVIDENCE"
		rep.Refuse = append(rep.Refuse, "no repetition directory was found")
	case len(modes) != 1:
		rep.Label = "REFUSED"
		rep.Refuse = append(rep.Refuse, fmt.Sprintf("repetitions disagree on the exporter: %v", modes))
	case modes["fake"]:
		// The fake exporter is the apparatus, not DCGM: nothing it scores is a result.
		rep.Label = "REHEARSAL"
	}
	// P5's failure is an unexpected change with its cause unresolved, never by itself proof that injection
	// reached NVML: the device plugin also marks devices unhealthy on its own NVML errors.
	rep.P5 = "held"
	for _, c := range cells {
		if c.Valid() && c.AllocatableChanged {
			rep.P5 = "FAILED: allocatable changed across an injection in " + filepath.Base(c.Dir) + "; cause unresolved"
		}
	}
	for _, arm := range Arms {
		v := ArmVerdict{Arm: arm, Summary: map[string][3]float64{}}
		var valid []CellResult
		for _, c := range cells {
			if c.Arm != arm {
				continue
			}
			v.Total++
			switch {
			case c.Valid():
				valid = append(valid, c)
			case c.Cut:
				v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, filepath.Base(c.Dir)+": cut by the session deadline")
			default:
				v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, filepath.Base(c.Dir)+": INVALID: "+strings.Join(c.Invalid, "; "))
			}
		}
		v.Valid = len(valid)
		switch {
		case notRun[arm]:
			v.Verdict = "NOT-RUN"
		case len(valid) < MinValidReps:
			v.Verdict = "UNJUDGED"
		case armInjection[arm].fault:
			v.Verdict = judgeFault(valid, &v)
		default:
			v.Verdict = "QUIET"
			for _, c := range valid {
				if len(c.Actions) > 0 {
					v.Verdict = "FALSE-POSITIVE"
					v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, filepath.Base(c.Dir)+": "+strings.Join(c.Actions, ", "))
				}
			}
			v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, fmt.Sprintf("%d fully observed window(s); this is a count, not a rate", len(valid)))
		}
		rep.Arms = append(rep.Arms, v)
	}
	return rep
}

func judgeFault(valid []CellResult, v *ArmVerdict) string {
	failed, outside := false, false
	for _, c := range valid {
		name := filepath.Base(c.Dir)
		for _, f := range c.Failed {
			failed = true
			v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, name+": "+f)
		}
		// P1 is breached only when the whole detect interval lies above the bound.
		if d := c.Hops["detect"]; d != nil && d.Lo > DetectBoundSeconds {
			outside = true
			v.Reasons = append(v.Reasons, fmt.Sprintf("%s: P1: detect [%.3f, %.3f] s lies above %.1f s", name, d.Lo, d.Hi, DetectBoundSeconds))
		}
	}
	// The per-hop summary: median, minimum and maximum of each interval's upper end, over valid repetitions.
	names := map[string]bool{}
	for _, c := range valid {
		for n := range c.Hops {
			names[n] = true
		}
	}
	for n := range names {
		var xs []float64
		for _, c := range valid {
			if h := c.Hops[n]; h != nil {
				xs = append(xs, h.Hi)
			}
		}
		sort.Float64s(xs)
		v.Summary[n] = [3]float64{median(xs), xs[0], xs[len(xs)-1]}
	}
	switch {
	case failed:
		return "FAILED"
	case outside:
		return "REACTED-OUTSIDE-BOUND"
	}
	return "REACTED"
}

func median(xs []float64) float64 {
	n := len(xs)
	if n%2 == 1 {
		return xs[n/2]
	}
	return (xs[n/2-1] + xs[n/2]) / 2
}

	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"

var gfT0 = time.Date(2026, 10, 27, 1, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

func gfScheme(t *testing.T) *runtime.Scheme {
	t.Helper()
	s := runtime.NewScheme()
	for _, add := range []func(*runtime.Scheme) error{clientgoscheme.AddToScheme, platformv1.AddToScheme, kueuev1beta1.AddToScheme} {
		if err := add(s); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	return s
}

func faultAssessment(xid, dbe int64) gpufault.Assessment {
	r := map[string]*gpufault.GPUReading{"GPU-a": {UUID: "GPU-a", GPU: "0", Values: map[string]float64{
		gpufault.FieldXid: float64(xid), gpufault.FieldECCDoubleBit: float64(dbe),
	}}}
	a, err := gpufault.Assess(r)
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return a
}

func TestDecideGPUFault(t *testing.T) {
	const stale = 5 * time.Second
	fault := faultAssessment(79, 0)
	fp := fault.Fingerprint()
	clean := faultAssessment(0, 0)
	missing := gpufault.Assessment{Cards: 1, Missing: []string{"GPU-a/" + gpufault.FieldECCDoubleBit}}
	obs := func(a gpufault.Assessment, age time.Duration) GPUFaultObservation {
		o := GPUFaultObservation{LastSuccess: gfT0.Add(-age), Assessment: a}
		if a.Fingerprint() != "" {
			o.FaultSince = gfT0.Add(-age)
		}
		return o
	}
	cases := []struct {
		name                 string
		o                    GPUFaultObservation
		hasObs               bool
		latched, ack         string
		signal, healthy      metav1.ConditionStatus
		healthyReason, latch string
		newLatch             bool
		taint                int
	}{
		{"no observation", GPUFaultObservation{}, false, "", "", metav1.ConditionFalse, metav1.ConditionUnknown, gpuHealthyReasonSignalNotFresh, "", false, 0},
		{"stale, nothing latched: no action", obs(fault, 6*time.Second), true, "", "", metav1.ConditionFalse, metav1.ConditionUnknown, gpuHealthyReasonSignalNotFresh, "", false, 0},
		{"stale, latched: the fault stays", obs(clean, 6*time.Second), true, fp, "", metav1.ConditionFalse, metav1.ConditionFalse, gpuHealthyReasonLatched, fp, false, +1},
		{"fresh fault latches", obs(fault, time.Second), true, "", "", metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionFalse, gpufault.ReasonXid, fp, true, +1},
		{"fresh fault already latched", obs(fault, 0), true, fp, "", metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionFalse, gpufault.ReasonXid, fp, false, +1},
		{"fresh clean clears", obs(clean, 0), true, fp, "", metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionTrue, gpuHealthyReasonNoFault, "", false, -1},
		{"acknowledged fault clears", obs(fault, 0), true, fp, fp, metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionTrue, gpuHealthyReasonAcknowledged, "", false, -1},
		{"a different fault is not covered by the acknowledgement", obs(faultAssessment(79, 1), 0), true, fp, fp, metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionFalse, gpufault.ReasonMultipleFault, faultAssessment(79, 1).Fingerprint(), true, +1},
		{"missing field, nothing latched: unknown, no action", obs(missing, 0), true, "", "", metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionUnknown, gpuHealthyReasonFieldMissing, "", false, 0},
		{"missing field, latched: the fault stays", obs(missing, 0), true, fp, "", metav1.ConditionTrue, metav1.ConditionFalse, gpuHealthyReasonLatched, fp, false, +1},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			d := decideGPUFault(c.o, c.hasObs, gfT0, stale, c.latched, c.ack, nil)
			if d.signal.Status != c.signal || d.healthy.Status != c.healthy || d.healthy.Reason != c.healthyReason ||
				d.latch != c.latch || d.newLatch != c.newLatch || d.taint != c.taint {
				t.Fatalf("got signal=%s healthy=%s/%s latch=%q new=%v taint=%d",
					d.signal.Status, d.healthy.Status, d.healthy.Reason, d.latch, d.newLatch, d.taint)
			}
			if c.newLatch && !d.observedAt.Equal(c.o.FaultSince) {
				t.Fatalf("observedAt %s, want the scrape that first carried the fault, %s", d.observedAt, c.o.FaultSince)
			}
		})
	}
}

// A failed scrape newer than the last success keeps the signal fresh until StaleAfter, and then names the failure.
func TestDecideGPUFaultNamesTheFailureOnceStale(t *testing.T) {
	o := GPUFaultObservation{LastSuccess: gfT0.Add(-6 * time.Second), Assessment: faultAssessment(0, 0),
		LastFailure: gfT0.Add(-time.Second), FailureReason: signalReasonScrapeFailed, FailureDetail: "connection refused"}
	d := decideGPUFault(o, true, gfT0, 5*time.Second, "", "", nil)
	if d.signal.Reason != signalReasonScrapeFailed || d.healthy.Status != metav1.ConditionUnknown {
		t.Fatalf("signal %s/%s healthy %s", d.signal.Status, d.signal.Reason, d.healthy.Status)
	}
	o.LastSuccess = gfT0.Add(-2 * time.Second)
	if d := decideGPUFault(o, true, gfT0, 5*time.Second, "", "", nil); d.signal.Status != metav1.ConditionTrue {
		t.Fatalf("one failure inside StaleAfter made the signal %s", d.signal.Status)
	}
}

// gfRig is one Ready node with a NodeHealth, a clock and a store, ready to reconcile.
type gfRig struct {
	t     *testing.T
	c     client.Client
	r     *NodeHealthReconciler
	store *GPUFaultStore
	now   time.Time
}

func newGFRig(t *testing.T, evict bool, objs ...client.Object) *gfRig {
	t.Helper()
	node := &corev1.Node{Name: "gpu-1", Status: corev1.NodeStatus{
		Conditions: []corev1.NodeCondition{{Type: corev1.NodeReady, Status: corev1.ConditionTrue}}}}
	nh := &platformv1.NodeHealth{Name: "nh-1", Finalizers: []string{nodeHealthFinalizer},
		Spec: platformv1.NodeHealthSpec{NodeName: "gpu-1"}}
	c := fake.NewClientBuilder().WithScheme(gfScheme(t)).
		WithIndex(&platformv1.NodeHealth{}, NodeNameIndex, indexNodeHealthByNodeName).
		WithIndex(&corev1.Pod{}, "spec.nodeName", func(o client.Object) []string { return []string{o.(*corev1.Pod).Spec.NodeName} }).
		WithStatusSubresource(&platformv1.NodeHealth{}, &kueuev1beta1.Workload{}).
		WithObjects(append([]client.Object{node, nh}, objs...)...).Build()
	g := &gfRig{t: t, c: c, store: NewGPUFaultStore(), now: gfT0}
	g.r = &NodeHealthReconciler{Client: c, Scheme: c.Scheme(), GPUFault: &GPUFaultConfig{
		Store: g.store, StaleAfter: 5 * time.Second, EvictWorkloads: evict, APIReader: c,
		Now: func() time.Time { return g.now },
	}}
	return g
}

func (g *gfRig) scrape(a gpufault.Assessment) {
	g.store.recordSuccess("gpu-1", g.now, a)
}

func (g *gfRig) reconcile() {
	g.t.Helper()
	if _, err := g.r.Reconcile(context.Background(), ctrl.Request{Name: "nh-1"}); err != nil {
		g.t.Fatalf("reconcile: %v", err)
	}
}

func (g *gfRig) state() (*platformv1.NodeHealth, *corev1.Node) {
	g.t.Helper()
	var nh platformv1.NodeHealth
	var node corev1.Node
	if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Name: "nh-1"}, &nh); err != nil {
		g.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Name: "gpu-1"}, &node); err != nil {
		g.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return &nh, &node
}

func TestGPUFaultLatchesTaintsAndStampsEachHop(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.now = gfT0.Add(30 * time.Millisecond)
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node := g.state()
	if !slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) {
		t.Fatalf("no gpu-fault taint: %v", node.Spec.Taints)
	}
	if nh.Status.Phase != phaseQuarantine || nh.Status.FaultSignal == nil || nh.Status.FaultSignal.Source != faultSourceDCGM {
		t.Fatalf("phase %s fault %+v", nh.Status.Phase, nh.Status.FaultSignal)
	}
	h := meta.FindStatusCondition(nh.Status.Conditions, conditionGPUHealthy)
	if h == nil || h.Status != metav1.ConditionFalse || h.Reason != gpufault.ReasonXid {
		t.Fatalf("GPUHealthy %+v", h)
	}
	// The observation time is the scrape's, not the reconcile's, and the taint time follows it.
	if got := nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultObservedAt]; got != gfT0.Format(time.RFC3339Nano) {
		t.Fatalf("observed-at %q", got)
	}
	if got := nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultTaintedAt]; got != g.now.Format(time.RFC3339Nano) {
		t.Fatalf("tainted-at %q", got)
	}
	if nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint] != "GPU-a/DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS=79" {
		t.Fatalf("fingerprint %q", nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint])
	}

	// A second reconcile with the same fault changes nothing it recorded.
	g.now = gfT0.Add(2 * time.Second)
	g.reconcile()
	nh2, _ := g.state()
	if nh2.Annotations[annGPUFaultTaintedAt] != nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultTaintedAt] || nh2.Status.LastTransitionTime.Time != nh.Status.LastTransitionTime.Time {
		t.Fatalf("a repeated reconcile moved a recorded time")
	}

	// A clean fresh scrape clears it and removes the taint, and only that taint.
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(0, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node = g.state()
	if slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) || nh.Status.Phase != phaseReady || nh.Status.FaultSignal != nil {
		t.Fatalf("not cleared: taints %v phase %s", node.Spec.Taints, nh.Status.Phase)
	}
	if nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint] != "" || nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultClearedAt] == "" {
		t.Fatalf("annotations after clear: %v", nh.Annotations)
	}
}

// The last-Xid field holds one Xid; an application Xid replacing a fatal one is not a repair and must not clear.
func TestGPUFaultApplicationXidAfterAFatalOneKeepsTheLatch(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	g.now = g.now.Add(time.Second)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(13, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node := g.state()
	if !slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) || nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint] == "" {
		t.Fatalf("Xid 13 after Xid 79 cleared the fault")
	}
	// Without a latch the same Xid 13 is no fault at all.
	g2 := newGFRig(t, false)
	g2.scrape(faultAssessment(13, 0))
	g2.reconcile()
	nh2, _ := g2.state()
	if h := meta.FindStatusCondition(nh2.Status.Conditions, conditionGPUHealthy); h == nil || h.Status != metav1.ConditionTrue {
		t.Fatalf("an application Xid alone: %+v", h)
	}
	// Zero, which a reset leaves, clears it.
	g.now = g.now.Add(time.Second)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(0, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	if nh, _ = g.state(); nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint] != "" {
		t.Fatalf("zero did not clear the latch")
	}
}

// A benign change is recorded as read, so a quiet control can be told from a blind one.
func TestGPUFaultRecordsTheReadingItIgnored(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	r := map[string]*gpufault.GPUReading{"GPU-a": {UUID: "GPU-a", Values: map[string]float64{
		gpufault.FieldXid: 0, gpufault.FieldECCDoubleBit: 0, gpufault.FieldECCSingleBit: 1}}}
	a, err := gpufault.Assess(r)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	g.scrape(a)
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node := g.state()
	if len(node.Spec.Taints) != 0 || !strings.Contains(nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultReading], gpufault.FieldECCSingleBit+"=1") ||
		nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultReadingAt] != gfT0.Format(time.RFC3339Nano) {
		t.Fatalf("taints %v annotations %v", node.Spec.Taints, nh.Annotations)
	}
}

// Stale data must neither set nor clear a fault: it is its own condition.
func TestGPUFaultStaleDataTakesNoAction(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.now = gfT0.Add(10 * time.Second)
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node := g.state()
	if slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) || nh.Status.Phase != phaseReady {
		t.Fatalf("a stale fault reading tainted the node")
	}
	s := meta.FindStatusCondition(nh.Status.Conditions, conditionGPUSignal)
	if s == nil || s.Status != metav1.ConditionFalse || s.Reason != signalReasonStale {
		t.Fatalf("GPUSignal %+v", s)
	}
	if h := meta.FindStatusCondition(nh.Status.Conditions, conditionGPUHealthy); h == nil || h.Status != metav1.ConditionUnknown {
		t.Fatalf("GPUHealthy %+v, want Unknown", h)
	}

	// Latched, then the exporter goes away: the fault stays latched and the taint stays on.
	g.now = gfT0.Add(20 * time.Second)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	g.store.recordSuccess("gpu-1", g.now, faultAssessment(0, 0)) // the clean reading is about to go stale
	g.now = g.now.Add(10 * time.Second)
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node = g.state()
	if !slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) || nh.Annotations[annGPUFaultFingerprint] == "" {
		t.Fatalf("stale data cleared a latched fault")
	}
}

// The path off is the reconciler as it was: no GPU conditions, no taint, whatever the store says.
func TestGPUFaultOffChangesNothing(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.r.GPUFault = nil
	g.reconcile()
	nh, node := g.state()
	if len(node.Spec.Taints) != 0 || meta.FindStatusCondition(nh.Status.Conditions, conditionGPUHealthy) != nil || nh.Status.Phase != phaseReady {
		t.Fatalf("the path acted while off: taints %v conditions %v", node.Spec.Taints, nh.Status.Conditions)
	}
}

func gpuPod(name, node string, jobUID types.UID, gpus string) *corev1.Pod {
	p := &corev1.Pod{Name: name, Namespace: "t", Spec: corev1.PodSpec{NodeName: node,
		Containers: []corev1.Container{{Name: "c", Resources: corev1.ResourceRequirements{}}}},
		Status: corev1.PodStatus{Phase: corev1.PodRunning}}
	if gpus != "" {
		p.Spec.Containers[0].Resources.Limits = corev1.ResourceList{gpuResourceName: resource.MustParse(gpus)}
	}
	if jobUID != "" {
		p.OwnerReferences = []metav1.OwnerReference{{APIVersion: "batch/v1", Kind: "Job", Name: "j", UID: jobUID, Controller: new(true)}}
	}
	return p
}

func workload(name string, jobUID types.UID) *kueuev1beta1.Workload {
	return &kueuev1beta1.Workload{Name: name, Namespace: "t",
		Labels: map[string]string{kueueJobUIDLabel: string(jobUID)},
		Status: kueuev1beta1.WorkloadStatus{Conditions: []metav1.Condition{
			{Type: kueuev1beta1.WorkloadQuotaReserved, Status: metav1.ConditionTrue, Reason: "QuotaReserved", LastTransitionTime: metav1.NewTime(gfT0)}}}}
}

func TestGPUFaultDeactivatesOnlyKueueGPUWorkOnTheFaultedNode(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, true,
		gpuPod("on-node", "gpu-1", "uid-1", "1"),
		gpuPod("elsewhere", "gpu-2", "uid-2", "1"),
		gpuPod("cpu-only", "gpu-1", "uid-3", ""),
		gpuPod("no-owner", "gpu-1", "", "1"),
		workload("wl-1", "uid-1"), workload("wl-2", "uid-2"), workload("wl-3", "uid-3"),
	)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(0, 1))
	g.reconcile()
	get := func(name string) *kueuev1beta1.Workload {
		var wl kueuev1beta1.Workload
		if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Namespace: "t", Name: name}, &wl); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return &wl
	}
	wl := get("wl-1")
	if ptr.Deref(wl.Spec.Active, true) || wl.Annotations[annWorkloadDeactivatedNode] != "gpu-1" || wl.Annotations[annWorkloadDeactivatedAt] == "" {
		t.Fatalf("the faulted node's GPU workload was not deactivated: active=%v %v", wl.Spec.Active, wl.Annotations)
	}
	for _, n := range []string{"wl-2", "wl-3"} {
		if w := get(n); !ptr.Deref(w.Spec.Active, true) {
			t.Fatalf("%s was deactivated", n)
		}
	}

	// Not reactivated until Kueue has evicted it and released the quota.
	g.reconcile()
	if wl = get("wl-1"); ptr.Deref(wl.Spec.Active, true) {
		t.Fatalf("reactivated before Kueue evicted it")
	}
	meta.SetStatusCondition(&wl.Status.Conditions, metav1.Condition{Type: kueuev1beta1.WorkloadEvicted, Status: metav1.ConditionTrue, Reason: "Deactivated"})
	meta.SetStatusCondition(&wl.Status.Conditions, metav1.Condition{Type: kueuev1beta1.WorkloadQuotaReserved, Status: metav1.ConditionFalse, Reason: "Pending"})
	if err := g.c.Status().Update(context.Background(), wl); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// The Pod is gone from the node by now, as the suspended Job's Pods are.
	if err := g.c.Delete(context.Background(), &corev1.Pod{Name: "on-node", Namespace: "t"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	g.reconcile()
	wl = get("wl-1")
	if !ptr.Deref(wl.Spec.Active, true) || wl.Annotations[annWorkloadReactivatedAt] == "" {
		t.Fatalf("not reactivated after eviction: active=%v %v", wl.Spec.Active, wl.Annotations)
	}
}

// A Workload a person deactivated carries no deactivated-node and must stay deactivated.
func TestGPUFaultNeverReactivatesAWorkloadItDidNotDeactivate(t *testing.T) {
	wl := workload("manual", "uid-9")
	wl.Spec.Active = new(false)
	wl.Status.Conditions = []metav1.Condition{{Type: kueuev1beta1.WorkloadEvicted, Status: metav1.ConditionTrue, Reason: "Deactivated", LastTransitionTime: metav1.NewTime(gfT0)}}
	g := newGFRig(t, true, wl)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(0, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	var got kueuev1beta1.Workload
	if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Namespace: "t", Name: "manual"}, &got); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if ptr.Deref(got.Spec.Active, true) {
		t.Fatalf("a manually deactivated workload was reactivated")
	}
}

// Without --gpu-fault-evict-workloads a fault taints and evicts nothing.
func TestGPUFaultDoesNotEvictUnlessAskedTo(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false, gpuPod("on-node", "gpu-1", "uid-1", "1"), workload("wl-1", "uid-1"))
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	var wl kueuev1beta1.Workload
	if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Namespace: "t", Name: "wl-1"}, &wl); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !ptr.Deref(wl.Spec.Active, true) {
		t.Fatalf("evicted without the flag")
	}
}

func TestGPUFaultTaintGoesWithTheNodeHealth(t *testing.T) {
	g := newGFRig(t, false)
	g.scrape(faultAssessment(79, 0))
	g.reconcile()
	nh, _ := g.state()
	if err := g.c.Delete(context.Background(), nh); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	g.r.GPUFault = nil // even with the path turned off since
	g.reconcile()
	var node corev1.Node
	if err := g.c.Get(context.Background(), types.NamespacedName{Name: "gpu-1"}, &node); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if slices.ContainsFunc(node.Spec.Taints, isGPUFaultTaint) {
		t.Fatalf("deleting the NodeHealth left its gpu-fault taint")
	}
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
#
# Runs hack/gpu-fault-session.sh against a stub aws, docker and make, without credentials, a card or money.
#
#   bash hack/test/check-gpu-fault-session.sh
#
# Modelled on hack/test/check-mps-capability-session.sh, whose stub it reuses in shape. What it pins:
# - every pre-purchase refusal stops before run-instances;
# - the launch is the pinned AMI on a g5.2xlarge at the $1.1324 cap, terminating on shutdown, carrying the sweeper's
#   tag at the request plus 6,940 s, rewritten to LaunchTime plus 6,970 s; LaunchTime is handed to the instance;
# - the rendered user-data has no placeholder, parses, carries the spliced bring-up, runs the instance procedure
#   under the registered phase arithmetic, and fits EC2's 16 KB;
# - the end: the instance is terminated, the evidence is scored by the real scorer, and an unconfirmed termination
#   or an instance on another image fails the session.
#
# The session runs from a throwaway commit of the working tree, because it refuses a dirty tree.
set -uo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." || exit 1

fails=0
ok() { echo "ok: $*"; }
bad() { echo "FAIL: $*"; fails=$((fails + 1)); }
work=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT

git ls-files -co --exclude-standard | grep -v '^hack/gpu-fault-20\|^hack/gpu-fault-rehearsal-\|^hack/m5c-20\|^hack/mpscap-' |
  tar -cf - -T - | tar -xf - -C "$work" || { echo "could not copy the tree"; exit 1; }
mkdir -p "$work/repo" && (cd "$work" && find . -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 ! -name repo -exec mv {} repo/ \;)
(cd "$work/repo" && git init -q && git add -A && git -c user.email=t@t -c user.name=t commit -qm test) || { echo "could not commit the copy"; exit 1; }
REPO="$work/repo"

mkdir -p "$work/bin"
# The aws stub of check-mps-capability-session.sh: rules matched in order, answers taken in turn, downloads from fixtures.
cat >"$work/bin/aws" <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import fnmatch, os, shutil, sys
stub = os.environ["STUB"]
args = sys.argv[1:]
line = " ".join(args)
with open(os.path.join(stub, "calls.log"), "a") as f:
    f.write(line + "\n")
if args[:2] == ["s3", "cp"]:
    paths = [a for a in args[2:] if not a.startswith("--")]
    if len(paths) == 2 and paths[0].startswith("s3://") and not paths[1].startswith("s3://"):
        key = paths[0].split("/", 3)[3]
        fx = os.path.join(stub, "fixtures", key.split("/", 1)[1] if "/" in key else key)
        if os.path.exists(fx):
            shutil.copy(fx, paths[1])
            sys.exit(0)
        sys.stderr.write("fatal error: An error occurred (404) when calling the HeadObject operation\n")
        sys.exit(1)
for i, rule in enumerate(open(os.path.join(stub, "rules")).read().splitlines()):
    if not rule:
        continue
    glob, files, rc = rule.split("\t")
    if fnmatch.fnmatchcase(line, glob):
        counter = os.path.join(stub, f"count.{i}")
        n = int(open(counter).read()) if os.path.exists(counter) else 0
        open(counter, "w").write(str(n + 1))
        seq = files.split("|")
        name = seq[min(n, len(seq) - 1)]
        if name != "-":
            sys.stdout.write(open(os.path.join(stub, name)).read())
        sys.exit(int(rc))
sys.exit(0)
EOF
# Images are not built here: make succeeds without building, and docker save writes a few bytes per image.
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "make $*" >>"$STUB/calls.log"\nexit 0\n' >"$work/bin/make"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "docker $*" >>"$STUB/calls.log"\n[ "$1" = save ] && echo "image $2"\nexit 0\n' >"$work/bin/docker"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' >"$work/bin/sleep"
chmod +x "$work/bin/"*

  put runs "6.0"; put errors "0.0"
  put creds "{\"Expiration\": \"$(date -u -d '+4 hours' +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"}"
  put none ""
  put subnet "subnet-0a"
  put iid "i-0gpufault"
  LT=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S+00:00)
  put launched "$LT	$AMI"
  put terminated "terminated"
}
base_rules() {
  rule "sts get-caller-identity*" account
  rule "ec2 describe-images*" ami
  rule "ec2 describe-spot-price-history*" prices
  rule "service-quotas get-service-quota*" quota
  rule "lambda get-function*" active
  rule "events describe-rule*" enabled
  rule "events list-targets-by-rule*" target
  rule "cloudwatch get-metric-statistics*Invocations*" runs
  rule "cloudwatch get-metric-statistics*Errors*" errors
  rule "configure export-credentials*" creds
  rule "ec2 describe-instances*tag:purpose*" none
  rule "ec2 describe-subnets*" subnet
  rule "ec2 run-instances*" iid
  rule "ec2 describe-instances*LaunchTime*" launched
  rule "ec2 describe-instances*State.Name*" terminated
}
# The evidence an instance whose smoke cell stopped the session would upload: real files, scored by the real scorer.
smoke_stopped_evidence() {
  local d="$STUB/ev/gf-run"
  mkdir -p "$d/reps"
  printf 'S1=pass\nstop=S4: field 311\narm_xid=skipped\narm_ecc=skipped\narm_benign=skipped\narm_none=skipped\n' >"$d/smoke.env"
  tar -czf "$STUB/fixtures/evidence.tgz" -C "$STUB/ev" gf-run
  echo nonce >"$STUB/fixtures/DONE"
}
session() {
  shift
  out="$(cd "$REPO" && env -i HOME="$HOME" PATH="$work/bin:$PATH" STUB="$STUB" GOCACHE="${GOCACHE:-$HOME/.cache/go-build}" \
    GOPATH="${GOPATH:-$HOME/go}" GOFLAGS=-mod=mod SESSION_CONFIRM=yes GPU_FAULT_AMI_ID="$AMI" OUT="$STUB/out" \
    SPOT_TERMINATE_WAIT=0 SPOT_TERMINATE_TRIES=2 "$@" timeout 600 bash hack/gpu-fault-session.sh 2>&1)"
  rc=$?
  printf '%s\n' "$out" >"$STUB/session.out"
}
launched() { grep -q '^ec2 run-instances' "$STUB/calls.log"; }
refused() {
  local name="$1" needle="$2"
  shift 2
  session "$name" "$@"
  if [ "$rc" = 0 ]; then bad "$name: the session exited 0"
  elif launched; then bad "$name: the session launched before refusing"
  elif [[ "$out" != *"$needle"* ]]; then bad "$name: no '$needle' in: $(tail -2 <<<"$out")"
  else ok "$name: refused before any launch"; fi
}

# --- the refusals ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
scene; base_rules; refused "no approval" "SESSION_CONFIRM=yes is required" SESSION_CONFIRM=
scene; base_rules; refused "no pinned AMI" "pins the AMI by ID" GPU_FAULT_AMI_ID=
scene; base_rules; refused "a caller's block count" "GF_BLOCKS is set" GF_BLOCKS=1
scene; base_rules; refused "a caller's instance type" "INSTANCE_TYPE is set" INSTANCE_TYPE=g5.xlarge
scene; put acct2 "123456789012"; rule "sts get-caller-identity*" acct2; base_rules; refused "another account" "paid sessions run only in gpu-lab"
scene; put ami2 "Ubuntu Server 22.04	available"; rule "ec2 describe-images*" ami2; base_rules; refused "an AMI that is not the GPU base image" "not an available Deep Learning Base"
scene; put dear $'ap-northeast-2a\t1.500000'; rule "ec2 describe-spot-price-history*" dear; base_rules; refused "a price over the ceiling" "at or under \$1.1324/h"
scene; put q4 "4.0"; rule "service-quotas get-service-quota*" q4; base_rules; refused "too little Spot quota" "needs 8"
scene; put inactive "Pending"; rule "lambda get-function*" inactive; base_rules; refused "a sweeper not Active" "is Pending, not Active"
scene; put few "2.0"; rule "cloudwatch get-metric-statistics*Invocations*" few; base_rules; refused "a sweeper seen running twice" "at least 4 clean runs"
scene; put errs "1.0"; rule "cloudwatch get-metric-statistics*Errors*" errs; base_rules; refused "a sweeper with an error" "1.0 error(s)"
scene; put soon "{\"Expiration\": \"$(date -u -d '+120 min' +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"}"; rule "configure export-credentials*" soon; base_rules
refused "credentials that expire before 155 minutes" "the session needs 155"
scene; put earlier "i-0earlier"; rule "ec2 describe-instances*tag:purpose*" earlier; base_rules; refused "an earlier instance still live" "still live (i-0earlier)"
scene; base_rules; mkdir -p "$STUB/out"; refused "an output directory that already exists" "already exists; each session writes a new directory"

# --- the whole session -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
scene; base_rules; smoke_stopped_evidence
t0=$(date +%s)
session "a whole session"
[ "$rc" = 0 ] && ok "a whole session exited 0" || bad "a whole session exited $rc: $(tail -4 "$STUB/session.out")"
grep -q "^GPU FAULT REACTION: NO-EVIDENCE" "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" 2>/dev/null && grep -qx "termination=confirmed" "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" &&
  ok "the real scorer read the returned evidence, and the termination is recorded" || bad "the verdict: $(head -3 "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" 2>/dev/null | paste -sd';')"
tag_at_launch=$(grep '^ec2 run-instances' "$STUB/calls.log" | grep -o 'Key=study-deadline,Value=[0-9TZ:-]*' | head -1 | sed 's/.*Value=//')
want_lo=$(( t0 + 6940 )); got=$(date -u -d "$tag_at_launch" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
(( got >= want_lo && got <= want_lo + 120 )) && ok "the launch carries the sweeper's tag at the request plus 6,940 s ($tag_at_launch)" || bad "the launch's tag is '$tag_at_launch'"
lt_epoch=$(date -u -d "$LT" +%s)
want_tag=$(date -u -d "@$(( lt_epoch + 6970 ))" +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)
grep -q "^ec2 create-tags .*--resources i-0gpufault --tags Key=study-deadline,Value=$want_tag" "$STUB/calls.log" &&
  ok "the tag is rewritten to LaunchTime plus 6,970 s ($want_tag)" || bad "the tag rewrite was: $(grep create-tags "$STUB/calls.log")"
grep -q '^s3 cp --only-show-errors .*launch-time.txt s3://gpufault-gpu-007635145730/.*/launch-time.txt' "$STUB/calls.log" &&
  ok "LaunchTime is handed to the instance through the run's prefix" || bad "LaunchTime was not uploaded"
grep -q '^ec2 run-instances .*--image-id ami-097edab977037f65f --instance-type g5.2xlarge .*--instance-initiated-shutdown-behavior terminate' "$STUB/calls.log" &&
  grep -q 'MaxPrice\\\?":\\\?"1.1324' "$STUB/calls.log" &&
  ok "the launch is the pinned AMI on a g5.2xlarge at the \$1.1324 cap, terminating on shutdown" || bad "the launch was: $(grep '^ec2 run-instances' "$STUB/calls.log" | cut -c1-300)"
for n in controller gpu-simulator job; do
  grep -q "^s3 cp --only-show-errors .*/images/$n.tar.gz s3://gpufault-gpu-007635145730/.*/images/$n.tar.gz" "$STUB/calls.log" ||
    bad "the $n image was not uploaded"
done
grep -q '^make docker-build IMG=controller:gpufault' "$STUB/calls.log" && grep -q '^make docker-build-gpu-simulator GPU_SIMULATOR_IMG=gpu-simulator:gpufault' "$STUB/calls.log" &&
  ok "both images are built from the committed tree" || bad "the image builds were not called"
grep -q '^ec2 terminate-instances .*i-0gpufault' "$STUB/calls.log" && ok "the instance is terminated" || bad "no terminate call"
ud="$STUB/out/user-data.sh"
# The patterns below are the user-data's own text, so its dollar signs are matched literally.
# shellcheck disable=SC2016
! grep -q '_PLACEHOLDER' "$ud" && ok "the user-data has no placeholder left" || bad "a placeholder survived"
bash -n "$ud" && ok "the user-data parses" || bad "the user-data does not parse"
grep -qx 'docker exec m5cgpu-worker systemctl restart containerd || exit 1' "$ud" && ok "the user-data carries the bring-up spliced from hack/m5c-gpu-session.sh" ||
  bad "the bring-up is not in the user-data"
# shellcheck disable=SC2016
grep -q 'P1=$(( L + 720 )); P2=$(( L + 1320 )); P3=$(( L + 6520 )); P4=$(( L + 6820 )); EV=$(( L + 6940 ))' "$ud" &&
  ok "the phases end at 720, 1,320, 6,520 and 6,820 s after LaunchTime and the evidence at 6,940 s" || bad "the phase arithmetic is not the registered one"
grep -q '( sleep 7000; shutdown -h now ) &' "$ud" && ok "the first backstop stops the instance at 7,000 s" || bad "no 7,000 s backstop"
# shellcheck disable=SC2016
grep -q 'GF_ARMS_END="$P3" GF_CHECKS_END="$P4"' "$ud" && grep -q 'bash hack/gpu-fault-instance.sh' "$ud" &&
  ok "the instance procedure runs under the phase deadlines" || bad "the instance procedure is not run with its deadlines"
(( $(wc -c <"$ud") < 16384 )) && ok "the user-data is $(wc -c <"$ud") bytes, under EC2's 16 KB" || bad "the user-data is $(wc -c <"$ud") bytes"

# --- the ways the end can go wrong -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
scene; put other "$LT	ami-0other"; rule "ec2 describe-instances*LaunchTime*" other; base_rules; smoke_stopped_evidence
session "an instance running another image"
[ "$rc" != 0 ] && [[ "$out" == *"runs ami-0other, not the pinned"* ]] && ok "an instance running another image is refused" || bad "another image was accepted (exit $rc)"
grep -q '^ec2 terminate-instances .*i-0gpufault' "$STUB/calls.log" && ok "and it is terminated at once" || bad "the wrong-image instance was not terminated"
scene; put running "running"; rule "ec2 describe-instances*State.Name*" running; base_rules; smoke_stopped_evidence
session "termination not confirmed"
[ "$rc" != 0 ] && grep -qx "termination=unconfirmed" "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" && ok "an unconfirmed termination fails the session and is recorded" ||
  bad "an unconfirmed termination: exit $rc, $(tail -1 "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" 2>/dev/null)"
scene; base_rules; echo nonce >"$STUB/fixtures/DONE"
session "no evidence came back"
grep -q "NO-EVIDENCE (the evidence archive did not come back)" "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" 2>/dev/null && ok "a session whose evidence never came back says so" ||
  bad "no evidence gave: $(head -1 "$STUB/out/verdict.txt" 2>/dev/null)"

if [[ $fails -ne 0 ]]; then
  echo "check-gpu-fault-session: $fails failure(s)"
  exit 1
fi
echo "check-gpu-fault-session: all checks passed"

package gpufault

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// scrape renders the three fields for one card the way dcgm-exporter 3.3.9 does, modelName comma included.
func scrape(xid, dbe, sbe string) []byte {
	l := `{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-aaaa",device="nvidia0",modelName="NVIDIA A10G, test",Hostname="n1"}`
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("# HELP DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS Value of the last XID error encountered.\n")
	b.WriteString("# TYPE DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS gauge\n")
	if xid != "" {
		b.WriteString(FieldXid + l + " " + xid + "\n")
	}
	if dbe != "" {
		b.WriteString(FieldECCDoubleBit + l + " " + dbe + "\n")
	}
	if sbe != "" {
		b.WriteString(FieldECCSingleBit + l + " " + sbe + "\n")
	}
	b.WriteString(`DCGM_FI_DEV_GPU_UTIL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-aaaa"} 97` + "\n")
	return []byte(b.String())
}

func assess(t *testing.T, body []byte) Assessment {
	t.Helper()
	r, err := ParseExposition(body)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("parse: %v", err)
	}
	a, err := Assess(r)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("assess: %v", err)
	}
	return a
}

func TestAssessHealthyCardHasNoFaultAndNothingMissing(t *testing.T) {
	a := assess(t, scrape("0", "0", "0"))
	if len(a.Faults) != 0 || len(a.Missing) != 0 || a.Cards != 1 || a.Fingerprint() != "" {
		t.Fatalf("healthy card read as %+v", a)
	}
}

func TestAssessFatalXidIsAFaultWithItsCode(t *testing.T) {
	a := assess(t, scrape("79", "0", "0"))
	if got := a.Fingerprint(); got != "GPU-aaaa/DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS=79" {
		t.Fatalf("fingerprint %q", got)
	}
	if a.Reason() != ReasonXid {
		t.Fatalf("reason %q", a.Reason())
	}
}

// The device plugin's application Xids are about a process, not the card, and must not quarantine a node.
func TestAssessApplicationXidsAreNotFaults(t *testing.T) {
	for _, x := range []string{"13", "31", "43", "45", "68"} {
		if a := assess(t, scrape(x, "0", "0")); len(a.Faults) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("Xid %s read as a fault: %+v", x, a.Faults)
		}
	}
	// 48 is the double-bit ECC Xid and is not in the plugin's list.
	if a := assess(t, scrape("48", "0", "0")); len(a.Faults) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("Xid 48 not read as a fault: %+v", a)
	}
}

func TestAssessDoubleBitCountIsAFaultAndSingleBitNever(t *testing.T) {
	if a := assess(t, scrape("0", "1", "0")); a.Reason() != ReasonECCDoubleBit || a.Fingerprint() != "GPU-aaaa/DCGM_FI_DEV_ECC_DBE_VOL_TOTAL=1" {
		t.Fatalf("double-bit read as %+v", a)
	}
	if a := assess(t, scrape("0", "0", "4000")); len(a.Faults) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("single-bit count read as a fault: %+v", a)
	}
	if a := assess(t, scrape("79", "2", "0")); a.Reason() != ReasonMultipleFault || len(a.Faults) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("two faults read as %+v", a)
	}
}

// A card that does not carry a fault field cannot be called healthy; the absence is reported, not filled in.
func TestAssessMissingAndUnavailableFieldsAreReported(t *testing.T) {
	a := assess(t, scrape("0", "", "0"))
	if len(a.Missing) != 1 || a.Missing[0] != "GPU-aaaa/"+FieldECCDoubleBit {
		t.Fatalf("missing %v", a.Missing)
	}
	a = assess(t, scrape("0", "N/A", "0"))
	if len(a.Missing) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("an unavailable value was read as present: %+v", a)
	}
	// A scrape with none of the fields has no cards, which the caller must not read as healthy.
	a = assess(t, []byte(`DCGM_FI_DEV_GPU_UTIL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-aaaa"} 97`+"\n"))
	if a.Cards != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("cards %d", a.Cards)
	}
}

func TestParseRefusesWhatItCannotRead(t *testing.T) {
	for name, body := range map[string]string{
		"no labels":       FieldXid + " 79\n",
		"no value":        FieldXid + `{UUID="GPU-a"}` + "\n",
		"no uuid":         FieldXid + `{gpu="0"} 79` + "\n",
		"label no equals": FieldXid + `{UUID="GPU-a",bad} 79` + "\n",
		"two values":      FieldXid + `{UUID="GPU-a",pod="x"} 79` + "\n" + FieldXid + `{UUID="GPU-a",pod="y"} 0` + "\n",
	} {
		if _, err := ParseExposition([]byte(body)); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: parsed without error", name)
		}
	}
	r, err := ParseExposition([]byte(FieldXid + `{UUID="GPU-a"} 2.5` + "\n"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := Assess(r); err == nil {
		t.Errorf("a fractional Xid was assessed")
	}
}

package controller

import (
	"context"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
	"k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/labels"
	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client"
	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/client/fake"
	"sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime/pkg/event"

	platformv1 "github.com/lkhun9311/gpu-mlops-platform-control-plane/api/v1"
)

// fakeExporter serves whatever body it is given, or a status code.
type fakeExporter struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	body   string
	status int
}

func (f *fakeExporter) set(body string, status int) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	f.body, f.status = body, status
}

func (f *fakeExporter) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, _ *http.Request) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	w.WriteHeader(f.status)
	_, _ = w.Write([]byte(f.body))
}

func exporterPod(name, node, ip string) *corev1.Pod {
	return &corev1.Pod{
		Name: name, Namespace: "sys", Labels: map[string]string{"app.kubernetes.io/component": "dcgm-exporter"},
		Spec: corev1.PodSpec{NodeName: node},
		Status: corev1.PodStatus{Phase: corev1.PodRunning, PodIP: ip,
			Conditions: []corev1.PodCondition{{Type: corev1.PodReady, Status: corev1.ConditionTrue}}},
	}
}

type sourceRig struct {
	src *GPUFaultSource
	exp *fakeExporter
	now time.Time
}

func newSourceRig(t *testing.T, extraPods ...client.Object) *sourceRig {
	t.Helper()
	exp := &fakeExporter{status: http.StatusOK}
	srv := httptest.NewServer(exp)
	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
	host, portStr, err := net.SplitHostPort(srv.Listener.Addr().String())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	port, _ := strconv.Atoi(portStr)
	nh := &platformv1.NodeHealth{Name: "nh-1", Spec: platformv1.NodeHealthSpec{NodeName: "gpu-1"}}
	objs := append([]client.Object{nh, exporterPod("exp-1", "gpu-1", host)}, extraPods...)
	c := fake.NewClientBuilder().WithScheme(gfScheme(t)).WithObjects(objs...).Build()
	g := &sourceRig{exp: exp, now: gfT0}
	g.src = &GPUFaultSource{
		Config: GPUFaultSourceConfig{Namespace: "sys", Selector: labels.SelectorFromSet(labels.Set{"app.kubernetes.io/component": "dcgm-exporter"}),
			Port: port, Interval: time.Second, Timeout: 800 * time.Millisecond, StaleAfter: 5 * time.Second},
		Reader: c, PodReader: c, Store: NewGPUFaultStore(), Events: make(chan event.GenericEvent, 16),
		HTTP: srv.Client(), Now: func() time.Time { return g.now },
	}
	return g
}

func (g *sourceRig) tick(t *testing.T) int {
	t.Helper()
	g.src.Tick(context.Background())
	n := 0
	for {
		select {
		case <-g.src.Events:
			n++
		default:
			return n
		}
	}
}

const (
	healthyBody = `DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 0
DCGM_FI_DEV_ECC_DBE_VOL_TOTAL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 0
DCGM_FI_DEV_ECC_SBE_VOL_TOTAL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 0
`
	faultBody = `DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 79
DCGM_FI_DEV_ECC_DBE_VOL_TOTAL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 0
`
)

func TestGPUFaultSourceWakesOnlyOnChangeAndStampsFirstSight(t *testing.T) {
	g := newSourceRig(t)
	g.exp.set(healthyBody, http.StatusOK)
	if n := g.tick(t); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("first tick woke %d times, want 1 (freshness gained)", n)
	}
	g.now = g.now.Add(time.Second)
	if n := g.tick(t); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("an unchanged scrape woke the reconciler %d times", n)
	}
	g.exp.set(faultBody, http.StatusOK)
	g.now = g.now.Add(time.Second)
	faultAt := g.now
	if n := g.tick(t); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("a new fault woke %d times", n)
	}
	g.now = g.now.Add(time.Second)
	g.tick(t)
	o, _ := g.src.Store.Get("gpu-1")
	if !o.FaultSince.Equal(faultAt) || o.Assessment.Fingerprint() != "GPU-a/DCGM_FI_DEV_XID_ERRORS=79" {
		t.Fatalf("FaultSince %s fingerprint %q; want the first scrape carrying it, %s", o.FaultSince, o.Assessment.Fingerprint(), faultAt)
	}
}

func TestGPUFaultSourceFailuresKeepTheLastReadingUntilStale(t *testing.T) {
	g := newSourceRig(t)
	g.exp.set(faultBody, http.StatusOK)
	g.tick(t)
	g.exp.set("", http.StatusInternalServerError)
	g.now = g.now.Add(2 * time.Second)
	if n := g.tick(t); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a failed scrape inside StaleAfter woke the reconciler")
	}
	o, _ := g.src.Store.Get("gpu-1")
	if o.FailureReason != signalReasonScrapeFailed || o.Assessment.Fingerprint() == "" {
		t.Fatalf("failure %q, reading %q", o.FailureReason, o.Assessment.Fingerprint())
	}
	g.now = g.now.Add(4 * time.Second)
	if n := g.tick(t); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("going stale woke the reconciler %d times, want 1", n)
	}
}

func TestGPUFaultSourceRefusesWhatItCannotAttribute(t *testing.T) {
	// Two exporters on one node: either could be wrong, so neither is read.
	g := newSourceRig(t, exporterPod("exp-2", "gpu-1", "127.0.0.2"))
	g.exp.set(faultBody, http.StatusOK)
	g.tick(t)
	o, _ := g.src.Store.Get("gpu-1")
	if o.FailureReason != signalReasonAmbiguous || !o.LastSuccess.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("two exporters: %+v", o)
	}
	// An exporter with none of the fields has said nothing about the cards.
	g = newSourceRig(t)
	g.exp.set(`DCGM_FI_DEV_GPU_UTIL{gpu="0",UUID="GPU-a"} 50`+"\n", http.StatusOK)
	g.tick(t)
	if o, _ := g.src.Store.Get("gpu-1"); o.FailureReason != signalReasonNoCards || !o.LastSuccess.IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("no fields: %+v", o)
	}
}

func TestGPUFaultSourceConfigRefusals(t *testing.T) {
	sel := labels.SelectorFromSet(labels.Set{"a": "b"})
	good := GPUFaultSourceConfig{Namespace: "n", Selector: sel, Port: 9400, Interval: time.Second, Timeout: 800 * time.Millisecond, StaleAfter: 5 * time.Second}
	if err := good.Validate(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for name, mut := range map[string]func(*GPUFaultSourceConfig){
		"empty selector":        func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.Selector = labels.Everything() },
		"timeout not < period":  func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.Timeout = time.Second },
		"stale not > period":    func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.StaleAfter = time.Second },
		"no namespace":          func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.Namespace = "" },
		"port out of range":     func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.Port = 0 },
		"non-positive interval": func(c *GPUFaultSourceConfig) { c.Interval = 0 },
	} {
		c := good
		mut(&c)
		if err := c.Validate(); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: accepted", name)
		}
	}
}
